Unfortunately, in realistic setups, the detection-efficiency mismatch can be significant. Here we present a more precise estimation of the secret key rate for the BB84 protocol with the passive basis choice, which accounts for the detection-efficiency mismatch between four threshold detectors as well as an adaptation of the decoy-state method. The suggested approach is used to estimate the secret key rate in a QKD experiment between the Micius satellite and the Zvenigorod ground station.

</details>


### [96] [From Barren Plateaus to SPSA Optimization in Variational Quantum Eigensolvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09810)
*Zhen Qin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了理论框架，表征了贫瘠高原（BP）现象对SPSA优化算法在有限次测量下的动力学影响，证明了BP导致的梯度能量指数衰减会使迭代次数和总测量预算呈指数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贫瘠高原现象是变分量子本征求解器（VQEs）可训练性的根本挑战，此前研究主要关注BP的几何起源，但其对有限次测量下实际算法的优化动力学和复杂度影响尚未被充分理解。

Method: 开发了一个理论框架：（1）推导了SPSA梯度估计器的非渐近偏差和方差刻画；（2）引入信噪比（SNR）分析来量化梯度可靠性；（3）在有限次测量下建立了SPSA的收敛保证。

Result: 研究表明，BP相关的梯度能量指数衰减导致达到固定相对优化精度所需的迭代次数呈指数增长，进而导致总测量预算呈指数增长。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了BP几何起源认知与实际算法复杂度分析之间的鸿沟，为理解BP对量子优化算法实际性能的影响提供了定量理论依据。

Abstract: The barren plateau (BP) phenomenon poses a fundamental challenge to the trainability of variational quantum eigensolvers (VQEs) by causing exponentially vanishing gradients as the system size increases. While extensive studies have investigated the geometric origins of BP, its impact on the optimization dynamics and complexity of practical algorithms under finite-shot measurements remains poorly understood. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework that characterizes how the BP affects the optimization dynamics of the Simultaneous Perturbation Stochastic Approximation (SPSA) algorithm and quantifies the resulting iteration complexity and measurement budget. We derive non-asymptotic bias and variance characterizations of the SPSA gradient estimator, introduce a signal-to-noise ratio analysis to quantify gradient reliability, and establish convergence guarantees for SPSA under finite-shot measurements. Our results show that the exponentially decaying gradient energy associated with BP leads to an exponential increase in the number of iterations required to achieve a fixed relative optimization accuracy, which in turn results in an exponential increase in the total measurement budget.

</details>


### [97] [Quantum Information Flow under String-Diagram Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09823)
*Yi-Yu Lin,Chen-Ye Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视Coecke早期提出的"量子信息流"概念，并为其给出显式的弦图形式化。通过区分表观通路与真正通路，定义了"Coecke流线"，并以ZX演算为例进行说明，将量子协议中的Coecke流解释为协议的约束性准局域线状呈现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息流这一概念在Coecke的早期工作中被提出，但缺乏严格的弦图形式化描述。本文旨在弥合这一理论空白，为量子信息流提供数学上严谨的形式化定义，使其可被精确分析和应用。

Method: 给定保持语义的弦图重写序列，区分依赖于当前图形表示的表观通路与可在连续重写中兼容继承至终极解耦裸线因子的真正通路；基于这种兼容继承关系形式化定义Coecke流线，并以ZX演算作为具体图示系统加以说明。

Result: 给出了Coecke流线的严格形式化定义，建立了通过重写序列继承通路的兼容继承关系，并在ZX演算框架中演示了该形式化的有效性。在量子协议背景下，Coecke流可被解释为协议弦图中目标裸线态射因子的约束性准局域线状呈现。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子信息流提供了严格的弦图理论形式化基础，使这一直觉性概念具备了可操作的定义，为后续在量子协议分析与ZX演算推理中的应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We revisit the notion of ``quantum information flow'' introduced in Bob Coecke's early work and seek to give it an explicit string-diagrammatic formalization. Given a semantics preserving string-diagram rewriting sequence, we first distinguish apparent through-paths, which depend on the current graphical presentation, from genuine through paths, which can be compatibly inherited through successive rewrites to a terminal decoupled bare wire factor. We then formally define a ``Coecke flow line'' in terms of this compatible inheritance relation.
  In particular, we use the ZX calculus, i.e., the ZX string diagram rewriting system, to illustrate the resulting formalism. In the physical setting of quantum protocols, a Coecke flow can be interpreted as a constrained, quasi-local, line-like presentation of a target bare wire morphism factor within the protocol string diagram.

</details>


### [98] [An upper bound for the purity of absolutely positive partial transpose states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09832)
*Anh T. Tran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了双量子比特系统中绝对可分态（absolutely separable states）与绝对正部分转置态（APPT states）的纯度上限问题，给出了APPT双体态纯度的上界，并在qubit-qudit系统中使其成为最优的紧致界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝对可分态和APPT态是量子信息理论中的重要概念，已知绝对可分态集合是APPT态集合的子集，且在qubit-qudit系统中二者相等。研究者希望通过纯度上界来刻画这两类态的边界特征，从而更深入地理解量子态的纠缠性质。

Method: 本文通过数学分析方法，推导了APPT双体量子态纯度的上界表达式。利用APPT态在任何全局酉变换下保持PPT性质这一约束条件，结合量子态的数学结构，给出了纯度（purity）的一个不等式上界。对于qubit-qudit系统，利用已知的绝对可分态与APPT态集合相等的性质，证明该上界为紧致的（即可以达到的最大值）。

Result: 给出了APPT双体态（从而也适用于绝对可分双体态）的纯度上界。在qubit-qudit系统中，该上界被证明是紧致的，即为APPT（及绝对可分）态纯度的最大值。

Conclusion: 该结果提供了一种通过纯度这一简单可计算的量来刻画APPT和绝对可分态的边界特征的方法，特别是在qubit-qudit系统中给出了精确的最大纯度，有助于量子态分类和纠缠检测方面的应用。

Abstract: A quantum state is called absolutely separable (resp. absolutely positive partial transpose (APPT)) if it remains separable (resp. positive partial transpose (PPT)) under any global unitary transformation. It is known that the set of all absolutely separable states is a subset of the set of all APPT states. Moreover, these two sets are identical for qubit-qudit systems. In this note, we give an upper bound for the purity of APPT bipartite states (and therefore for the purity of absolutely separable bipartite states). For qubit-qudit systems, this upper bound becomes the maximum purity of APPT (and absolutely separable) states.

</details>


### [99] [Tensor network methods for non-perturbative dynamics of open quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09850)
*Thibaut Lacroix,Adam Burgess,Nicola Lorenzoni,Julian Wiercinski,Kian Damezin,James Lim,Dario Tamascelli,Alex W. Chin,Moritz Cygorek,Brendon W. Lovett,Jonathan Keeling,Susana F. Huelga,Martin B. Plenio,Erik M. Gauger*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用张量网络方法实现对开放量子系统动力学的非微扰、数值精确求解，克服了传统马尔可夫主方程方法在记忆核指数缩放上的计算瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统动力学超越微扰处理（通常与马尔可夫主方程相关）的描述因记忆核不利的指数缩放而计算上极具挑战，亟需新的方法框架来克服这一瓶颈。

Method: 系统梳理了近年来在量子信息和凝聚态物理背景下发展的张量网络方法，这些方法提供了新的形式体系和工具箱，能够构建非微扰、数值精确的开放量子系统动力学描述方案，并控制数值精度。

Result: 展示了张量网络框架如何突破此前开放量子系统动力学的计算瓶颈，实现了可控数值精度下非微扰、数值精确的动力学求解方法。

Conclusion: 通过比较各类张量网络方法的共性与差异，提供了对该领域的全面视角，为该领域后续研究与应用奠定综述基础。

Abstract: The description of open quantum system dynamics beyond the perturbative treatment (usually associated with Markovian master equations) is a computationally challenging task due to the unfavorable exponential scaling of memory kernels. Developed over recent decades in the context of quantum information and condensed matter, tensor networks provide both a new formalism and a toolbox for overcoming previous computational bottlenecks. This framework enables the formulation of non-perturbative, numerically exact methods for describing the dynamics of open quantum systems to controllable numerical accuracy. In this review, we present these methods and discuss their commonalities and differences to paint a comprehensive view of the field.

</details>


### [100] [From Nonlinear Stochastic Differential Equations to Quantum Channels: The Kolmogorov--Lindblad Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09903)
*Hsuan-Cheng Wu,Xiantao Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种精确的Kolmogorov–Lindblad映射(KLM)，将非线性随机微分方程(SDE)的概率定律编码为迹为一的量子密度算子，建立了一个随机薛定谔方程描述的纯态过程，其平均态满足Lindblad方程，从而将经典SDE与开放量子动力学统一在同一量子计算框架下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性SDE在分子建模、药物发现、量化金融、随机学习和不确定性量化中至关重要，但其非线性漂移和对噪声实现的平均阻碍了直接的量子表示。如何在量子计算机上高效且精确地模拟经典随机动力学是一个亟待解决的问题。

Method: KLM方法在概率定律层面进行映射：将路径密度表示为半密度，其演化满足随机薛定谔方程；对纯态进行平均得到Lindblad方程，其对角核恰好是Fokker–Planck密度；经典扩散通过厄米跳算子的退相干表示；统计可观测量变为量子期望值；采用保持结构的Galerkin投影在有限维度维持Lindblad形式。

Result: 双势阱Langevin动力学和含噪Lorenz–63系统的数值实验展示出统计可观测量的快速收敛。该方法避免了直接幅值编码密度所需的未知时间相关归一化，并为经典二时间关联提供了精确的量子回归公式。

Conclusion: KLM提供了一条从一般非线性随机动力学到量子信道且数学可控的路径，将函数逼近和相干算子访问隔离为影响算法效率的剩余决定性因素，为经典SDE与开放量子系统的统一量子原生计算奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Nonlinear stochastic differential equations (SDEs) underlie molecular modeling and drug discovery, quantitative finance, stochastic learning, and uncertainty quantification. Their expectations, event probabilities, and time correlations are therefore natural targets for quantum computation, but nonlinear drift and averaging over noise realizations obstruct a direct quantum representation. We develop an exact \emph{Kolmogorov--Lindblad mapping} (KLM) at the level of the probability law, encoded natively as a trace-one quantum density operator. For each Brownian realization, the pathwise density admits a half-density whose evolution is a stochastic Schrödinger equation. Averaging the associated pure states yields a Lindblad equation for $Γ(t)$ whose diagonal kernel is exactly the Fokker--Planck density, $p(t,x)=Γ(t;x,x)$, with classical diffusion represented by decoherence through Hermitian jump operators. Statistical observables become quantum expectations without the unknown time-dependent normalization introduced by direct amplitude encoding of the density, and classical two-time correlations admit an exact quantum regression formula. A structure-preserving Galerkin projection retains the Lindblad form at finite dimension, placing classical SDEs and open quantum dynamics on the same quantum-native computational footing. Numerical experiments for double-well Langevin dynamics and noisy Lorenz--63 exhibit rapid convergence of statistical observables. KLM thus provides a mathematically controlled path from general nonlinear stochastic dynamics to quantum channels, while isolating function approximation and coherent operator access as the remaining determinants of algorithmic efficiency.

</details>


### [101] [Learning Clifford-structured quantum unitaries and Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09912)
*Arkopal Dutt,Dale Jacobs,John Jeang,Saeed Mehraban,Vladimir Podolskii*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究稠密于Pauli基但具有简洁Clifford分解的n量子比特酉算子与哈密顿量的学习问题，提出一种agnostic的Clifford酉量子层析协议，并据此扩展了可学习哈密顿量的范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的结构化量子酉算子与哈密顿量的学习算法主要局限于在Pauli基下具有局部性或稀疏性的过程。然而实际中可能存在一类在Pauli基下稠密（稀疏度可达Ω(2^n)）但具有简洁Clifford分解的量子过程，这类过程目前缺乏有效的学习算法。本文旨在填补这一空白，扩展可学习量子过程的类别。

Method: 本文首先引入一个agnostic的Clifford酉量子层析协议：给定对未知酉算子U的查询访问，以及最优Clifford保真度opt，该协议输出一个保真度至少为opt-ε的Clifford酉算子，时间复杂度为poly(n, (1/ε)^{log(1/ε)})。随后将该协议应用于具有有界Clifford extent（即Σ|α_i|有界）的酉算子U=Σα_i C_i和哈密顿量的层析学习中。

Result: 获得了针对具有有界Clifford extent的酉算子和哈密顿量的层析协议，将哈密顿量的可学习性从稀疏Pauli分解扩展到Pauli基下稠密但具有Clifford结构的情形。

Conclusion: 本文通过设计高效的agnostic Clifford酉层析协议，成功将量子过程的可学习性从Pauli稀疏类推广到Pauli稠密但Clifford结构化的一类，显著扩展了量子层析的适用范围。

Abstract: Learning algorithms for structured quantum unitaries and Hamiltonians have primarily considered classes of processes that are local or sparse in the Pauli basis. We turn our attention to learning $n$-qubit quantum unitaries $U$ and Hamiltonians $H$, given query access to $U$ or the unitary evolution of $H$, that may be dense in the Pauli basis but still admit concise Clifford decompositions. Specifically, we consider unitaries (or Hamiltonians) of the form $U = \sum_i α_i C_i$ over Cliffords $C_i$ with bounded Clifford extent $\sum_i |α_i|$. To extract this Clifford structure, we introduce an agnostic tomography protocol for Clifford unitaries that given query access to an unknown unitary $U$ with optimal Clifford fidelity $\textsf{opt}$, outputs a Clifford unitary witnessing fidelity $\geq \textsf{opt} - \varepsilon$ for some error $\varepsilon > 0$, in time $\textsf{poly}(n,(1/\varepsilon)^{\log(1/\varepsilon)})$. We then apply this protocol to obtain tomography protocols for unitaries and Hamiltonians that have bounded Clifford extent. This extends learnability of Hamiltonians from those with sparse Pauli decompositions to those that are dense (i.e., has sparsity $Ω(2^n)$) in the Pauli basis but are Clifford structured.

</details>


### [102] [From Koszul-Complex Stabilizer Models to Superselection Profiles: Topological Rigidity and Nonsplit Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09913)
*Hao Song*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了拓扑超选择剖面$\mathscr S_\sigma$，用于统一研究平移对称性增强拓扑（SET）相中的Koszul复形稳定子模型。通过同调代数工具（Ext、Tor、Schanle引理），建立了单层条件、刚性定理和粗粒化准则，并揭示了层间胶合携带单个层不可见的SET数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的稳定子编码模型（如toric-code层次和bivariate-bicycle码）属于同一同调框架，但缺乏统一的平移对称性SET相分析工具。现有方法仅关注单个同调层，无法捕捉跨层信息且难以刻画层间胶合效应。

Method: 引入拓扑超选择剖面$\mathscr S_\sigma=\tau_{\geq1}\mathbf R\mathrm{Hom}_R(\overline{\mathrm{coker}\varphi_\sigma},R)$，其上同调层$E_\sigma^\ell=\mathrm{Ext}_R^\ell(\overline{\mathrm{coker}\varphi_\sigma},R)$编码扇区、融合和平移作用。利用矩阵级Schanle引理证明刚性定理，借助Tor计算有限尺寸Z逻辑算子，并比对3D量子比特toric码的split与非split扩张。

Result: 对于正则Koszul模型，$E_\sigma^\ell$可显式计算且$\mathscr S_\sigma$为单层；素数qudit情形下刚性假设自动满足，有限层的平移核给出精确的粗粒化。构造了所有可容许单层数据；对3D qubit toric码的八个扩张模型，虽然$E_\sigma^\ell$层及平移作用完全相同，但其尺寸依赖的地面态简并度不同，表明层间胶合携带SET信息。

Conclusion: 层间胶合是SET相分类中不可约化的信息载体，仅依赖单个同调层不足以完整区分SET相。该框架统一了toric码层级与bivariate-bicycle码，为平移不变CSS码的SET相分类提供了严格的同调代数工具。

Abstract: Koszul-complex stabilizer models unify the toric-code hierarchy and bivariate-bicycle codes in one homological framework. To study translation-symmetry-enriched topological (SET) phases in these models and, more generally, in translation-invariant Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) codes with stabilizer maps $(\varphi_X,\varphi_Z)$, we introduce the associated topological superselection profile $\mathscr S_σ:=τ_{\geq1}\mathbf R\!\operatorname{Hom}_R(\overline{\operatorname{coker}\varphi_σ},R),\ σ=X,Z$. Its cohomology layers $E_σ^\ell:=H^\ell(\mathscr S_σ)\cong_R\operatorname{Ext}_R^\ell(\overline{\operatorname{coker}\varphi_σ},R)$ encode sectors, fusion, and translation action. When finite, the $\ell=1,2,\ldots$ layers describe pointlike, looplike, and higher-dimensional excitations; $\mathscr S_σ$ retains inter-layer gluing. For regular Koszul models, we compute $E_σ^\ell$ explicitly, find $\mathscr S_σ$ single-layer, and obtain finite-size $Z$-logicals from $\operatorname{Tor}$. Single-layer means that at most one positive-degree layer $E_σ^\ell$ is nonzero. Our matrix-level Schanuel-lemma method proves rigidity: under a finite-resolution hypothesis, the nonzero layer and degree uniquely determine a topological CSS code's translation SET order at the stable translation-invariant Clifford level. For prime qudits the hypothesis is automatic, and a finite layer's translation kernel gives the exact minimal coarse graining to a toric-code stack. We realize admissible single-layer data. Beyond this regime, split and nonsplit extensions of 3D qubit toric codes yield eight models sharing all identical $E_σ^\ell$ layers with trivial translation action but having distinct size-dependent ground-state degeneracies, hence distinct translation SET phases. Thus inter-layer gluing carries SET data invisible to individual layers.

</details>


### [103] [Decoupling 2D translation-invariant topological CSS codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09915)
*Yifei Wang,Zhongyi Ni,Mingxin He,Jinguo Liu,Yingfei Gu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了二维平移不变拓扑CSS码在最大任意子保留超晶格下总可以通过局域幺正变换解耦为环面码与乘积态的叠层，消除了此前关于进一步对称性阻碍的可能性，并给出了高效构造算法及超胞大小和算子扩散边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已知二维平移不变拓扑CSS码在粗粒化后局域等价于环面码叠层，但现有构造通常破坏了比去除任意子置换平移所需的更多平移对称性，因此不清楚是否存在更基本的阻碍阻止在保留最大对称性的情况下完成解耦。

Method: 通过传递到最大任意子保留超晶格，证明每个这样的码都能通过局域幺正变换解耦为环面码和乘积态；提出了高效的显式构造解耦映射的算法，并给出了所需的超胞大小和算子扩展的界；在一般情况下解耦不需要额外的辅助比特，且可扩展到具有适当边界条件的有限系统。

Result: 证明了不存在进一步阻碍：在最大任意子保留超晶格下，每个二维平移不变拓扑CSS码均可局域幺正解耦为环面码与乘积态；提供了高效构造算法及超胞大小和算子扩散的理论界。

Conclusion: 该结果完全解决了二维平移不变拓扑CSS码与其对应环面码叠层之间的局域等价问题，确认了最大对称性保留情况下解耦总是可能的，并为实际构造提供了算法工具。

Abstract: Two-dimensional translation-invariant topological CSS codes on qubits are known to be locally equivalent, after coarse-graining, to stacks of toric codes. However, existing constructions generally break more translation symmetry than is required to remove anyon-permuting translations, leaving open whether any further obstruction exists. We prove that no such obstruction occurs: after passing to the maximal anyon-preserving superlattice, every such code admits a local unitary decoupling into toric codes and product states. We further provide an efficient algorithm for explicitly constructing the decoupling map, together with bounds on the required supercell size and operator spreading. The decoupling requires no additional ancillas in generic cases and extends to finite systems with suitable boundary conditions.

</details>

### [104] [SuperEM: A Sub-meV Threshold Detector Architecture for Cosmic Neutrino Background and Dark Matter Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08592)
*Zhenjie Li,Xilei Sun,Xiaoshan Jiang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SuperEM（超导耦合半导体电子倍增）的新型探测器架构，通过超导吸收体与强偏置半导体P-N结的耦合，绕过传统辐射探测中"不可能三角"（亚meV能量阈值、宏观时间响应、空间可扩展性三者不可兼得）的限制，以实现宇宙中微子背景（CνB）直接探测、绝对中微子质量测量和亚GeV轻暗物质搜索所需的性能。信号输运仿真表明，未掺杂界面配合强漂移电场可实现纳秒级瞬态电子漂移，完整解析时间在35 ns以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前粒子物理、天体物理和宇宙学的前沿研究（CνB直接探测、绝对中微子质量测定、亚GeV轻暗物质搜寻）需要同时具备亚毫电子伏特（sub-meV）能量阈值、优异绝对能量分辨率、快速时间响应和大规模可扩展性的探测器，但现有技术面临"不可能三角"的根本性瓶颈——优化sub-meV阈值必然牺牲宏观时间响应或空间可扩展性。

Method: SuperEM架构的核心创新在于：将超导吸收体的超低能量阈值与强偏置高密度半导体P-N结的内在高增益数字化能力相耦合。入射能量在超导吸收体中产生大量非平衡准粒子，这些准粒子通过超薄原子层沉积（ALD）绝缘势垒进行量子隧穿提取，随后在半导体结中实现电子倍增。基于先前深度低温雪崩力学的实验验证，本文确立了该完整器件的理论可行性与结构基础，并通过信号输运仿真验证了未掺杂界面+强漂移场方案可实现高效、纳秒级瞬态电子漂移。

Result: 信号输运仿真结果证实：未掺杂界面与强漂移电场的组合能够实现高效、纳秒级尺度的瞬态电子漂移，信号完整解析时间在35 ns内完成，验证了SuperEM架构在时间响应方面的可行性。

Conclusion: SuperEM架构为下一代CνB和轻暗物质观测站提供了一种兼具可扩展性、高分辨率和快速时间响应的新型探测器框架，有望突破传统辐射探测器的固有局限性。

Abstract: Expanding the operational boundaries of radiation detection is imperative for contemporary particle physics, astrophysics, and cosmology. At this frontier, the direct detection of the Cosmic Neutrino Background (C$ν$B), the determination of the absolute neutrino mass scale, and the search for sub-GeV Light Dark Matter (LDM) necessitate detector architectures capable of sub-millielectronvolt (sub-meV) energy thresholds, exceptional absolute energy resolution, fast time response, and massive scalability. Current technologies confront an intrinsic limit---the ``impossible triangle''---wherein optimizing for sub-meV thresholds inherently compromises either macroscopic timing response or spatial scalability. Here, we introduce the Superconductor-Coupled Semiconductor Electron-Multiplying (SuperEM) detector, a fundamentally novel structural paradigm designed to bypass this limitation. The architecture couples the ultra-low energy threshold of a superconducting absorber with the intrinsic high-gain digitization of a strongly biased, high-density semiconductor P-N junction. Incident energy yields a proliferation of non-equilibrium quasiparticles, which are subsequently extracted via quantum tunneling across an ultra-thin Atomic Layer Deposition (ALD) insulating barrier. Building upon our prior empirical validation of deep-cryogenic avalanche mechanics, this manuscript establishes the fundamental theoretical feasibility and structural foundation of the complete device. Signal transport simulations confirm that an undoped interface coupled with a strong drift field enables highly efficient, nanosecond-scale transient electron drift, resolving completely within 35 ns. The SuperEM architecture thus constitutes a scalable, high-resolution, and fast time-response framework for next-generation C$ν$B and LDM observatories.

</details>


### [105] [Silent coverage failures in rare-event searches and a degeneracy index that predicts them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09203)
*Davide Pagno*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究低本底实验中模型偏差对六种区间估计程序覆盖概率的影响，提出了泊松-费雪简并指数I_PF(δν;θ0)=(β,γ)，用于量化信号平移和未被吸收残差，并证明在模拟器标定下无法抵御数据生成过程与模拟器的错配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低本底实验中，新物理搜索需要从少量事件中推断非负信号强度并报告上限。标称频率学派覆盖概率要求区间构造有效且数据模型充分，但实际中数据生成过程可能与模型存在偏差。需要系统研究模型偏差如何影响区间端点覆盖，以及如何诊断和缓解这种失配。

Method: 在精确泊松计数实验、暗物质反冲谱和无中微子双β衰变峰搜索中，对六种区间程序引入受控模型偏差，考察下/上端点覆盖。提出泊松-费雪简并指数I_PF，将期望计数变形投影到完整拟合切空间，得到有符号拟合信号偏移β（以剖面标准误为单位）和未吸收残差的泊松-费雪范数γ。β的符号指示受威胁端点，γ越大则饱和泊松拟合优度检验（5%类型I错误）的可检测性越强。

Result: 正向信号类偏差降低发现侧覆盖但使上限保守；来自信号效率高估的负向信号偏差可使上端点欠覆盖。标称模拟器下的校准无法抵御相对数据生成过程的模型错配。β大且γ小的变形难以被诊断，需以带辅助信息约束的 nuisance 表示或纳入可辩护包络。在精确共线约束nuisance基准中，对变形建模可在评估网格点恢复覆盖，但以可量化的区间灵敏度损失为代价。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了模型错配下区间覆盖的脆弱性，提供了系统化的诊断工具（泊松-费雪简并指数）和缓解策略（nuisance约束建模或包络方法），为低本底实验中的统计推断提供了重要的稳健性指导。

Abstract: Searches for new physics in low-background experiments infer a non-negative signal strength from few events and often report an upper limit. Nominal frequentist coverage requires both a valid interval construction and an adequate data model. We study how controlled model departures affect lower- and upper-endpoint coverage for six interval procedures in an exact Poisson counting experiment, dark-matter recoil spectra, and a neutrinoless-double-beta-decay peak search. We introduce the Poisson--Fisher degeneracy index $\mathcal I_{\mathrm{PF}}(δν;\vartheta_0)=(β,γ)$, which maps a specified expected-count deformation, after projection onto the complete fitted tangent space, to $β$, the signed fitted signal shift in profiled standard-error units, and $γ$, the Poisson--Fisher norm of the unabsorbed residual. Locally, the sign of $β$ identifies the threatened endpoint, while larger $γ$ implies greater detectability by the saturated-Poisson goodness-of-fit test used here at a fixed $5\%$ type-I error rate. Across the studied deformations, positive signal-like bias degrades discovery-side coverage while making upper limits conservative; negative signal bias from overestimated signal efficiency can make the upper endpoint undercover. Calibration under the nominal simulator does not protect against misspecification of that simulator relative to the data-generating process. A plausible deformation with large $|β|$ and small $γ$ may therefore evade diagnosis and should be represented by a nuisance constrained with auxiliary information or included in a defensible envelope. In the exactly collinear constrained-nuisance benchmark, modelling the deformation restores coverage at the evaluated grid points, at a quantifiable cost in interval sensitivity.

</details>


### [106] [Amplitude analysis for charmed meson decays at BESIII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09338)
*Han Zhang,Zhenxuan Li,Chunyi Guan,Zehui Lu,Hui Li,Liping Yang,Yechun Yu,Nan Zhang,Minggang Zhao,Yu Lu,Bai-Cian Ke,Liaoyuan Dong*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文全面综述了BESIII合作组在粲介子多体衰变中采用的振幅分析方法，涵盖概率密度函数与似然函数构建、蒙特卡洛归一化、探测效率和分辨率修正、基于XGBoost的多维背景建模，以及强子衰变和半轻子衰变的振幅形式体系，并详细讨论了拟合分数计算、运动学投影生成和统计不确定度估计等关键分析环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振幅分析是连接粲介子多体衰变实验测量与中间两体过程理论预言之间的关键桥梁，但缺乏对实际实现方法的系统性综述，尤其是BESIII实验的实践经验尚未得到全面总结。

Method: 文章系统介绍了无分箱最大似然拟合中概率密度函数和似然函数的构建方法，包括蒙特卡洛积分归一化、探测效率与分辨率效应的引入、基于XGBoost分类器的多维背景建模，以及标准和中间共振传播子参数化的振幅形式体系（涵盖强子衰变和半轻子衰变），并阐述了拟合分数评估、运动学投影生成和统计不确定度估计等关键分析工具。

Result: 提供了一套完整、实用的BESIII合作组振幅分析框架，覆盖从似然函数构建到最终物理结果的完整分析流程。

Conclusion: 该综述为粲介子多体衰变振幅分析提供了系统的实用指南，有助于标准化分析流程并促进实验与理论之间的有效对接。

Abstract: Amplitude analysis bridges the gap between experimental measurements of multibody charmed-meson decays and theoretical predictions of intermediate two-body processes. This work presents a comprehensive overview of the amplitude-analysis methodology employed by the BESIII Collaboration, emphasizing practical implementation. We detail the construction of the probability density function and likelihood function for unbinned maximum-likelihood fits. This encompasses Monte Carlo integration techniques for normalization, the incorporation of detection efficiency and resolution effects, and multidimensional background modeling utilizing XGBoost classifiers. Furthermore, we describe the amplitude formalism for both hadronic and semileptonic decays, incorporating standard resonance-propagator parametrizations. Key analytical aspects, including the evaluation of fit fractions, the generation of kinematic projections, and the estimation of statistical uncertainties, are also discussed.

</details>


### [107] [Automated Signal Integrity Analysis Framework for High-Speed Interconnects in the PPCB-1347-MuPix11 Probe Card](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09462)
*D M S Sultan,R. Plackett,A. E. McDougall,A. J. A. Knight,A. S. Rotelli,J. Vossebeld,D. Bortoletto*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可复用的MATLAB信号完整性（SI）分析框架，将四端口S参数数据转换为可追踪的链路级证据。该框架在PPCB-1347-MuPix11探针卡的四个1.25 Gbps差分路由上进行了验证，表明路径长度并非足够的SI排序变量，并提供了从设计评审到校准VNA测量解释的扩展基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高速互连信号完整性分析往往仅依赖单一的损耗指标（如插入损耗）来评估链路性能，缺乏从S参数到完整链路级证据的系统性转化方法。路径长度本身不足以作为SI排序依据，需要一个能够综合反映波形行为、模型投影和有限记录观测结果的自动化分析框架。

Method: 该框架包含自动化流程：1) 预检文件结构；2) 功率归一化的混合模式变换（mixed-mode transformation）；3) 路由长度感知的损耗分解；4) 构建因果加载的信道模型；5) 将完整PRBS-31序列传播至眼图、条件BER和8b10b编码链路分析。数据来源于PTSL CST Microwave 3D-Solver的四端口S参数和虚拟时域求解器。

Result: 在1.25 Gbps数据率（奈奎斯特频率0.625 GHz）下，DP1-DP4差分插入损耗(SDD21)范围为-0.350至-0.300 dB，差分转共模转换范围为-31.038至-28.236 dBc，FEB输入端眼图张度为0.586至0.588 V。分析表明链路长度不是唯一决定因素：DP3奈奎斯特插入损耗最低，DP1差分转共模隔离最强，DP4眼图最大。所有BER值均低于报告下限，不支持BER排序。

Conclusion: 路径长度单独不足以作为SI排序变量，需要在多个维度上综合评估链路性能。该框架通过区分路由相关波形行为、模型投影和有限记录观测，为高速互连SI比较分析提供了可扩展的、从设计到测量验证全过程的基础。

Abstract: A reusable MATLAB signal-integrity (SI) framework is presented that converts compatible four-port S-parameter data, measured by VNA or obtained from electromagnetic simulation, into traceable link-level evidence rather than a single loss metric. The framework is demonstrated on the four 1.25 Gbps differential routes (DP1-DP4) of the PPCB-1347-MuPix11 probe card using PTSL CST Microwave 3D-Solver-derived four-port S-parameters and a virtual time-domain solver. The automated pipeline preflights file structures, performs a power-normalized mixed-mode transformation, applies route-length-aware loss decomposition, constructs a causally loaded channel model, and propagates full PRBS-31 sequences into eye-diagram, conditional-BER, and 8b10b-coded-link analyses. At the 1.25 Gbps data rate (Nyquist 0.625 GHz), DP1-DP4 exhibit differential insertion loss (SDD21) from -0.350 to -0.300 dB, differential-to-common conversion from -31.038 to -28.236 dBc, and modeled FEB-input eye openings from 0.586 to 0.588 V. The comparison shows that path length alone is not an adequate SI ranking variable: DP3 has the lowest Nyquist insertion loss, DP1 the strongest differential-to-common isolation, and DP4 the largest modeled eye. All analytical BER values remain below the reporting floor and therefore do not support a BER ranking. By preserving the distinction between route-dependent waveform behavior, model projections, and finite-record observations, the framework provides an extensible basis for comparative high-speed-interconnect SI analysis from design review through calibrated VNA measurement interpretation.

</details>
